# About this item

*    230-Degree Pivoting Head – The head pivots smoothly and continuously through a 230-degree arc 
*    Internal Spring – An internal spring controls the tension of the head, allowing it to hold its position while you work 
*    AISI 4140 Steel Head – The breaker bar head is made of AISI 4140 steel and heat-treated to be stronger and more ductile than the handle 
*    Stronger External Head – Our external head design better resists spreading, twisting, or fracturing than an internal head design 
*    Sleek, Comfortable Handle – The finger groove has pronounced interior edges for traction and control for any finger placement

## Product Description

Tekton breaker bars have heads that pivot smoothly and continuously through a 230-degree arc. An internal spring controls the tension of the head, allowing it to hold its position while you work. The head is made of AISI 4140 steel and heated to be stronger and more ductile than the handle. Our external head design resists spreading, twisting, or fracturing better than an internal head design. Tekton breaker bars exceed industry standards for Proof Torque, a measure of strength verified by a single-cycle test that applies torque until the specified value is reached without failure or permanent deformation. These breaker bars have a sleek, comfortable handle with a finger groove that has pronounced interior edges for traction and control for any finger placement. The pommel at the end of the handle keeps your hand from slipping off and is rounded for comfort.

The size and weight receive mixed feedback, with several noting it's pretty big and heavy, while the flexibility is also mixed, with some reporting significant flex under load.

*   [![Image 91](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) Victor Hoyt](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AGXD4JF476K4RB5PYYJBAM54PSYQ?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [Hands down the nicest breaker bar on Amazon with the best warranty!!](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/R1F1808OF692FD/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on September 1, 2023 [Size: 1/2 Inch Drive x 24 Inch](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B07GH97P36/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. This breaker bar is hands down the nicest breaker bar on Amazon at the current moment. I ordered a few different brands before I decided including, tekton, capri, Michael pro, epauto, and the neiko, because let's be real, it's Amazon, so I can easily return the others. The cheapest ones being the neiko and epauto, which are around $27 or $28 and they have absolutely no warranty. Let's be real ppl, breaker bars break!! They are meant for the nitty gritty and take a beat down in a professional setting, or even a diy scenario. For $7 or $8 more you can get a capri, which does have a lifetime warranty... if you were the one who purchased it, and can show proof of purchase. The finish on the capri was terrible though, it felt like sand paper, and like I might get a metal shard in my hand at any second. The Michael pro was along the same lines, but it wasn't quite as bad as the capri though. The grinding of the bar wasn't the best, and there were portions that were not uniform, but the chrome finish wasn't to shabby. The michael pro was only about 22" from the drive to the handle tip, so not really a full 24" as advertised. The Michael pro wasn't terrible for the money though and it does come with a lifetime warranty, again, if you can show proof of purchase. For just a few dollars more though, you can get the tekton, which is a true 24" length from the drive end to the handle tip, and the chrome finish on it is perfect!! The bar is perfectly uniform, and I also noticed the drive end was just a few millimeters shorter than the others, which makes it a bit more stout, and allows for more torque before shearing the anvil. The bar has a perfect chrome finish and is just a sexy tool from head to toe!! The warranty... doesn't matter if you bought it, if someone else bought it, if you found it on the street, or if you broke it by misusing it. Tekton will have you a new one in 3 days!!! Tekton could care less about proof of purchase, which shows that they actually stand behind their tools. Spend the extra few dollars and get the tekton. It's worth it in every way!!! Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. This came as a set with the half inch torque wrench I bought. This pair is a perfect match. One to get honkin' nuts/bolts off, the other to get honkin' nuts/bolts back on (correctly). Although I use the torque wrench more often, I have only had a couple of times that I actually needed to use the power this breaker bar can deliver. It has amazing leverage. And although it bent farther than I ever thought it would, it didn't break (thank god as it would have probably hurt somehow). And it went right back to straight too. I don't really use this breaker bar enough to help any professional mechanics know how good it is, but for what I use it for around the garage and the house, it is more than enough tool for my applications. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. What I loved was this tool was able to loosen the nuts on the bumper bolts of my 4Runner so I could install the bull bar. Tried every tool I had, socket wrenches, wrenches but they just wouldn't budge so I stopped because I didn't want to wear down the edges of the nuts and turned to the Toyota forums for an answer which 2 people provided me with and that was to use a breaker bar not a torque wrench plus a good amount of lubricant. Which did the trick, 18" was more than enough to work with 24" would have been too long. You still have to use your sockets so just be sure if you're getting the right one which was 1/2" in my case. There are some tools you may only use just once but you'll be glad you have it in your arsenal of tools should you or someone you know need it. So satisfying to feel it finally loosen those nuts.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))       One person found this helpful Helpful  Sending feedback...Thank you for your feedback. ## About this Item

*   230-Degree Pivoting Head – The head pivots smoothly and continuously through a 230-degree arc
*   Internal Spring – An internal spring controls the tension of the head, allowing it to hold its position while you work
*   AISI 4140 Steel Head – The breaker bar head is made of AISI 4140 steel and heat-treated to be stronger and more ductile than the handle
*   Stronger External Head – Our external head design better resists spreading, twisting, or fracturing than an internal head design
*   Sleek, Comfortable Handle – The finger groove has pronounced interior edges for traction and control for any finger placement

## Product Description

 Tekton breaker bars have heads that pivot smoothly and continuously through a 230-degree arc. An internal spring controls the tension of the head, allowing it to hold its position while you work. The head is made of AISI 4140 steel and heated to be stronger and more ductile than the handle. Our external head design resists spreading, twisting, or fracturing better than an internal head design. Tekton breaker bars exceed industry standards for Proof Torque, a measure of strength verified by a single-cycle test that applies torque until the specified value is reached without failure or permanent deformation. These breaker bars have a sleek, comfortable handle with a finger groove that has pronounced interior edges for traction and control for any finger placement. The pommel at the end of the handle keeps your hand from slipping off and is rounded for comfort.
